This experiment instead relaxes the continuum of roots L_i M with L_i one of the 47 supports and M any nonzero real homogeneous linear form. The linear suffix may use arbitrary real homogeneous linear forms. By restriction to normalized t+B+D=1, the same relaxation covers affine linear factors/descendants there.

## 1. Quadratic directed-germ lifting (general theorem)

For the open active interval from a to b, choose the positive projective representative p(e)=(1-e)a+e b, v=b-a, 0<e<1. A real homogeneous quadratic Q with symmetric coefficient matrix A has exact germ sign lexsgn(a^T A a, 2a^T A v, v^T A v). These are three linear functionals of the six independent entries of A. Their joint zero is exactly the identity Q(p(e))=0 on that whole line segment, not just a point zero; in a normalized chart the corresponding support line divides the restricted polynomial (unless identically zero). For a product of linear forms L,M the eventual sign is the product of their eventual lex signs, including zero. The entire ordered tuple is retained before the sign readout. This follows from the first nonzero coefficient of the polynomial in e and the product sign law, and does not identify any distinct native X6 state.

For any finite fixed bank of directed germs, build the finite coefficient hyperplane arrangement from all three lifted coefficient rows per germ in the six-dimensional auxiliary coefficient space. Each attainable sign vector of the rows gives an exact germ partition, including all boundary signs. Conversely every quadratic has such a partition. A fully exact enumeration option is to enumerate row ternary sign patterns with rational linear feasibility: impose equality for zero rows and s_j r_j·A >= 1 for nonzero rows. Homogeneity and finite positive minimum prove equivalence to strict feasibility after a common positive scaling; for the all-zero pattern require a nonzero vector in the common nullspace if excluding the zero query. The same construction with two rows in dimension three gives the linear bank; R229 supplies the efficient exact rank-three face enumeration. No claim that all 3^m patterns need be enumerated. The degree-two bank and the degree-one bank are fixed under passage to subsets of the same germs. For arbitrary placement of the first joint observation, define mixed recursion H(S,d,k) by all attainable partitions, strict child masks only, each linear query costing zero joint budget and each quadratic one; H(empty,d,k)=true, nonempty at d=0 false, k<0 false. This is exact for the declared finite germ-elimination relaxation, and every actual correct tree induces a feasible germ tree, since finitely many eventual strict ancestor signs hold simultaneously on a nonempty local interval. A zero germ is discarded only as a lower-bound relaxation. Hence infeasibility certifies an actual obstruction; feasibility is not an output algorithm. This is a finite route for general arbitrary quadratic coefficients and interior first joint nodes, not an executed six-dimensional enumeration.

## 2. Exact root-family reduction and capacity

Use the 98 global directed endpoint germs of all 49 pinned components, before clipping by root. Enumerate every linear strict partition (A,B) by R229's complete rank-three endpoint sign arrangement, modulo global sign. For support i with strict partition (C,D), the product-root partition is exactly ((A∩C)∪(B∩D),(A∩D)∪(B∩C)), modulo swap; zeros vanish in this relaxation. Thus the entire continuum of possible second factors is represented in a finite set of root germ partitions. This representation preserves the eventual product signs on each retained germ but can omit wall obligations in the interior of a component after cutting. Count labels, not germs; split components of the same label count once. A pure-linear subtree of remaining depth five can have at most 31 labels. Its next two strict branches can each have at most 15. If its starting label count is exactly 31, its next query must discharge a label: otherwise each of the 31 tags survives in at least one child (a germ zero means annihilation on its supporting line). A nonzero linear form annihilates a germ iff proportional to its tag normal; at most one label can be discharged, so test only the 47 support normals in the saturated case. Otherwise test the complete 2225 partition bank. This is an exhaustive one-step necessary test, not a full depth-five recursion.

The exact mask count is the number of active two-germ components minus one for each of the two split tags with both components active. The program cross-checks it against direct label-mask counts on all linear partitions and their unions before scanning. Canonical unordered masks are valid here because child depth budgets and label constraints are symmetric. Different second-factor classes can coincide after product; counts distinguish pairs of support index and linear germ partition from distinct product-root masks. None of these are counts of individual real polynomials.

## 4. Reproduce (Node.js, no dependencies)

```javascript
function scan(data){
const dot=(a,b)=>a.reduce((s,x,i)=>s+x*b[i],0n),cross=(a,b)=>[a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]],abs=x=>x<0n?-x:x,gcd=(a,b)=>b?gcd(b,a%b):abs(a),norm=(a,un=false)=>{let g=a.reduce(gcd,0n);return g?a.map(x=>x/(un&&a.find(Boolean)<0n?-g:g)):a},sg=x=>x>0n?1:x<0n?-1:0;
const N=data.map(e=>e.normal.map(BigInt)),parts=data.map(e=>e.normal.join()==="51,2,-1"?[[["1","9","69"],["3","17","187"]],[["2","5","112"],["1","0","51"]]]:e.normal.join()==="17,-1,0"?[[["0","0","1"],["1","17","391"]],[["1","17","69"],["1","17","22"]]]:[e.endpoints]).map(s=>s.map(pair=>pair.map(p=>p.map(BigInt))));
let P=[],idx=new Map(),G=[],tag=Array(47).fill(0n),components=[],even=0n;
function id(p){p=norm(p);let k=p.join();if(!idx.has(k)){idx.set(k,P.length);P.push(p)}return idx.get(k)}
for(let k=0;k<parts.length;k++)for(let [a,b] of parts[k]){if(dot(N[k],a)||dot(N[k],b))throw Error("endpoint");let u=id(a),v=id(b);if(u===v)throw Error("degenerate");let first=1n<<BigInt(G.length);components.push([k,first]);even|=first;for(let [i,j] of [[u,v],[v,u]]){let bit=1n<<BigInt(G.length);G.push([i,j,bit]);tag[k]|=bit;}}
if(N.length!==47||components.length!==49||G.length!==98)throw Error("dimensions");
let rays=new Map();for(let i=0;i<P.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<P.length;j++){let v=norm(cross(P[i],P[j]),true);if(v.some(Boolean))rays.set(v.join(),v)}
if(![...rays.values()].some(v=>P.some(p=>dot(v,p))))throw Error("rank");
const canonical=(a,b)=>a<b?[a,b]:[b,a],mask=s=>{let a=0n,b=0n;for(let [u,v,bit] of G){let z=s[u]||s[v];if(z>0)a|=bit;else if(z<0)b|=bit}return canonical(a,b)};
let qs=new Map(),signs=new Set();function add(s){let key=s.join();if(signs.has(key))return;signs.add(key);let [a,b]=mask(s);qs.set(a+","+b,[a,b])}
for(let v of rays.values()){let sv=P.map(p=>sg(dot(v,p)));add(sv);for(let i=0;i<P.length;i++)if(!sv[i]){let w=cross(v,P[i]),sw=P.map((p,j)=>sv[j]?0:sg(dot(w,p))),su=P.map((p,j)=>sv[j]||sw[j]?0:sg(dot(P[i],p)));for(let e of [-1,1]){add(sv.map((z,j)=>z||e*sw[j]));for(let f of [-1,1])add(sv.map((z,j)=>z||e*sw[j]||f*su[j]))}}}
let Q=[...qs.values()],S=N.map(n=>mask(P.map(p=>sg(dot(n,p))))),dup=tag.filter((_,k)=>parts[k].length===2).map(t=>t&even);
if(dup.length!==2)throw Error("dup");
function pc32(x){x=x-((x>>>1)&0x55555555);x=(x&0x33333333)+((x>>>2)&0x33333333);return (((x+(x>>>4))&0x0f0f0f0f)*0x01010101)>>>24}
function pc(s){return pc32(Number(s&0xffffffffn))+pc32(Number((s>>32n)&0xffffffffn))+pc32(Number((s>>64n)&0xffffffffn))+pc32(Number(s>>96n))}
function count(s){let a=(s|(s>>1n))&even;let n=pc(a);for(let d of dup)if((a&d)===d)n--;return n}
function slow(s){let n=0;for(let t of tag)if(t&s)n++;return n}
for(let [a,b] of Q.concat(S))for(let t of [a,b,a|b])if(count(t)!==slow(t))throw Error("count mismatch");

for(let i=0;i<S.length;i++){let [a,b]=S[i],good=0,bad=0;for(let [c,d] of Q){let [x,y]=canonical((a&c)|(b&d),(a&d)|(b&c));if(count(x)>31||count(y)>31){bad++;continue}good++;let k=x+","+y;if(!roots.has(k))roots.set(k,[x,y,0]);roots.get(k)[2]++}per.push([i,bad,good]);direct+=bad;total+=good+bad}
let hist={},checks=0,sm=new Map();
const ordered=[...new Map([...S,...Q].map(([a,b])=>[a+","+b,[a,b]])).values()];
function one(s){if(sm.has(s))return sm.get(s);let c=count(s),ok=false;if(c<=15)ok=true;else{let bank=c===31?S:ordered;for(let [a,b] of bank){checks++;if(count(a&s)<=15&&count(b&s)<=15){ok=true;break}}}sm.set(s,ok);return ok}
let excluded=0,excludedPairs=0,remaining=[],hist2={};
for(let [a,b,m] of roots.values()){let k=Math.max(count(a),count(b));hist[k]=(hist[k]||0)+1;let [x,y]=count(a)>=count(b)?[a,b]:[b,a];if(!one(x)||!one(y)){excluded++;excludedPairs+=m}else{remaining.push([a,b,m]);hist2[k]=(hist2[k]||0)+1}}
const pre={points:P.length,germs:G.length,rays:rays.size,signPatterns:signs.size,linearPartitions:Q.length,total,direct,passed:total-direct,distinctPassed:roots.size,hist,per,oneStep:{excluded,excludedPairs,remaining:remaining.length,remainingPairs:total-direct-excludedPairs,checks,states:sm.size,hist:hist2}};
return {summary:pre,remaining:remaining.map(([a,b,m])=>[a.toString(16),b.toString(16),m])};
}
```
